\displaystyle B_{3}\leq\frac{o_{\varepsilon}(1)}{\Lambda_{\varepsilon}(1+|y_{\varepsilon}|)^{\tau}}\Big(\frac{|y_{\varepsilon}|^{p^{\prime}}d\eta}{|\eta|^{p^{\prime}}}\Big)^{\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}}\leq C\frac{|y_{\varepsilon}|^{\frac{2-p^{\prime}}{p^{\prime}}}}{(1+|y_{\varepsilon}|)^{\tau}}=o_{\varepsilon}(1).

(2.59)

Together they imply that

\displaystyle B=o_{\varepsilon}(1).

(2.60)

(
2.55
) and (
2.60
) imply that

A+B=o_{\varepsilon}(1)
.
We get a contradiction.

Case 3.
|y_{\varepsilon}|
is unbounded and
|y_{\varepsilon}|\simeq\varepsilon^{-1}
.

We can draw the contradiction in a similar way. Since the large part of the argument is the same, we only point out the difference.
As in
[
5
, Proposition 3.1]
, we get

\displaystyle|G_{\varepsilon}(y_{\varepsilon},\eta)-G_{\varepsilon}(0,\eta)|\leq C\Big(|\ln|\eta||+|\ln|y_{\varepsilon}-\eta||+\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Big).

Notice that

\displaystyle\int_{B_{\frac{2\delta}{\varepsilon}}(0)}

\displaystyle\Big|G_{\varepsilon}(y_{\varepsilon},\eta)-G_{\varepsilon}(0,\eta)\Big|\times\Big\{\Big|\sum_{j=1}^{n}a_{ij}e^{v_{j}(\eta)}\widetilde{w}^{*}_{t,i,\varepsilon}(\eta)\Big|+\Big|\frac{\varepsilon_{t}^{2}\widetilde{g}^{0}_{i,\varepsilon}(\eta)}{\Lambda_{\varepsilon}\cdot\varepsilon^{-\alpha}O_{m^{*}}(1+|y_{\varepsilon}|)^{\tau}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{t=1}^{N}\frac{r_{3,t}\widetilde{Z}_{3,t,i,\varepsilon}^{*}(\eta)}{\Lambda_{\varepsilon}\cdot\varepsilon^{-\alpha}O_{m^{*}}(1+|y_{\varepsilon}|)^{\tau}}\cdot\sum_{s=1}^{N}\widetilde{\chi}_{s}(\eta)e^{v_{i}(\eta+\frac{p_{t,\varepsilon}-p_{l,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{\varepsilon_{l}}}

\displaystyle\quad+s_{i}\sum_{s=1}^{N}\frac{\widetilde{\chi}_{s}(\eta)e^{v_{i}(\eta+\frac{p_{t,\varepsilon}-p_{l,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{\varepsilon_{l}}}}{\Lambda_{\varepsilon}\cdot\varepsilon^{-\alpha}O_{m^{*}}(1+|y_{\varepsilon}|)^{\tau}}\Big|\Bigg\}d\eta

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg\{C\int_{B_{\frac{2\delta}{\varepsilon}(0)}}\Big(|\ln|\eta||+|\ln|y_{\varepsilon}-\eta||+\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Big)\Big|\sum_{j=1}^{n}a_{ij}e^{v_{j}(\eta)}\widetilde{w}^{*}_{t,i,\varepsilon}(\eta)\Big|d\eta\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad+\Bigg\{C\int_{B_{\frac{2\delta}{\varepsilon}(0)}}\Big(|\ln|\eta||+|\ln|y_{\varepsilon}-\eta||+\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Big)\times

\displaystyle\quad\times\Big|\frac{\varepsilon_{t}^{2}\widetilde{g}^{0}_{i,\varepsilon}(\eta)}{\Lambda_{\varepsilon}\cdot\varepsilon^{-\alpha}O_{m^{*}}(1+|y_{\varepsilon}|)^{\tau}}+\sum_{t=1}^{N}\frac{r_{3,t}\widetilde{Z}_{3,t,i,\varepsilon}^{*}(\eta)}{\Lambda_{\varepsilon}\cdot\varepsilon^{-\alpha}O_{m^{*}}(1+|y_{\varepsilon}|)^{\tau}}\cdot\sum_{s=1}^{N}\widetilde{\chi}_{s}(\eta)e^{v_{i}(\eta+\frac{p_{t,\varepsilon}-p_{l,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{\varepsilon_{l}}}

\displaystyle\quad+s_{i}\sum_{s=1}^{N}\frac{\widetilde{\chi}_{s}(\eta)e^{v_{i}(\eta+\frac{p_{t,\varepsilon}-p_{l,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{\varepsilon_{l}}}}{\Lambda_{\varepsilon}\cdot\varepsilon^{-\alpha}O_{m^{*}}(1+|y_{\varepsilon}|)^{\tau}}\Big|d\eta\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=:A^{\prime}+B^{\prime}.

As in
Step 2
, by (
2.38
) and (
2.39
), we get

\displaystyle A^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq C\int_{B_{\frac{2\delta}{\varepsilon}}(0)}\frac{|\ln|\eta||d\eta}{(1+|y_{\varepsilon}|)^{\tau}(1+|\eta|)^{m^{*}-\tau}}+C\int_{B_{\frac{2\delta}{\varepsilon}}(0)}\frac{|\ln|y_{\varepsilon}-\eta||d\eta}{(1+|y_{\varepsilon}|)^{\tau}(1+|\eta|)^{m^{*}-\tau}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+C\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\cdot\int_{B_{\frac{2\delta}{\varepsilon}}(0)}\frac{d\eta}{(1+|y_{\varepsilon}|)^{\tau}(1+|\eta|)^{m^{*}-\tau}}

Here,

\displaystyle\int_{B_{\frac{2\delta}{\varepsilon}}(0)}\frac{|\ln|\eta||d\eta}{(1+|y_{\varepsilon}|)^{\tau}(1+|\eta|)^{m^{*}-\tau}}\leq\frac{C}{(1+|y_{\varepsilon}|)^{\tau}}\int_{0}^{\frac{2\delta}{\varepsilon}}\frac{r|\ln r|dr}{(1+r)^{m^{*}-\tau}}=o_{\varepsilon}(1),

\displaystyle\int_{B_{\frac{2\delta}{\varepsilon}}(0)}\frac{|\ln|y_{\varepsilon}-\eta||d\eta}{(1+|y_{\varepsilon}|)^{\tau}(1+|\eta|)^{m^{*}-\tau}}

\displaystyle=\int_{B_{1}(y_{\varepsilon})}\frac{|\ln|y_{\varepsilon}-\eta||d\eta}{(1+|y_{\varepsilon}|)^{\tau}(1+|\eta|)^{m^{*}-\tau}}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\int_{B_{\frac{2\delta}{\varepsilon}}(0)\backslash B_{1}(y_{\varepsilon})}\frac{|\ln|y_{\varepsilon}-\eta||d\eta}{(1+|y_{\varepsilon}|)^{\tau}(1+|\eta|)^{m^{*}-\tau}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{|y_{\varepsilon}|^{m^{*}}}+\frac{\ln\frac{C}{\varepsilon}}{(1+|y_{\varepsilon}|)^{\tau}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{d\eta}{(1+|\eta|)^{m^{*}-\tau}}

\displaystyle=o_{\varepsilon}(1)

and

\displaystyle\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\cdot\int_{B_{\frac{2\delta}{\varepsilon}}(0)}\frac{d\eta}{(1+|y_{\varepsilon}|)^{\tau}(1+|\eta|)^{m^{*}-\tau}}\leq\frac{\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}{(1+|y_{\varepsilon}|)^{\tau}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\frac{d\eta}{(1+|\eta|)^{m^{*}-\tau}}.

Together they imply

\displaystyle A^{\prime}=o_{\varepsilon}(1).

On the other hand, we get

\displaystyle B^{\prime}\leq C\Big(\int_{B_{\frac{2\delta}{\varepsilon}}(0)}|\ln|\eta||^{p^{\prime}}+|\ln|y_{\varepsilon}-\eta||^{p^{\prime}}+|\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon}|^{p^{\prime}}\Big)^{\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}}\cdot\frac{o_{\varepsilon}(1)}{(1+|y_{\varepsilon}|)^{\tau}}\leq\frac{C}{\varepsilon^{\frac{2+\theta}{p^{\prime}}}}\frac{1}{(1+|y_{\varepsilon}|)^{\tau}}=o_{\varepsilon}(1)

if we let
\theta
small enough and
p
sufficiently closed to
1
.

Therefore, for any case, we get a contradiction. Then,
\Lambda_{\varepsilon}\to\infty
and Proposition

2.13
is proved.

\Box

In such a way, we obtain a function defined in
\cup_{t=1}^{N}B_{3\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}})
. Then, we get a
C^{2}
0-extension
w_{i,\varepsilon}^{0}
defined on
\Omega
with

(1)

\|w^{0}_{i,\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}=O_{m^{*}}\varepsilon^{-\alpha-\tau}
;

(2)

w^{0}_{i,\varepsilon}|_{B_{2\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}=R^{0}_{t,i,\varepsilon}(|x-p_{t,\varepsilon}|)
for any
t=1,\cdots,N
;

(3)

w^{0}_{i,\varepsilon}|_{\Omega\backslash\cup_{t=1}^{N}B_{3\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}=0
.

2.6
Refined Estimates on the Error Term II: Parts of
w_{\varepsilon}
with non-zero frequency

2.6.1
Parts of
w_{\varepsilon}
with Frequency no less than 2

Now we consider the parts of
w_{\varepsilon}
with frequency no less than 2. By the Fourier expansion, the
k
-frequency part of
w_{i,\varepsilon}
is defined by

\displaystyle w_{t,i,\varepsilon}^{k}:=\sin(k\theta)\cdot R_{1,t,i,\varepsilon}^{k}(r)+\cos(k\theta)\cdot R_{2,t,i,\varepsilon}^{k}(r)

with

\displaystyle-\Big(\frac{d^{2}}{dr^{2}}+\frac{1}{r}\frac{d}{dr}-\frac{k^{2}}{r^{2}}\Big)\Big(\sum_{l=1}^{n}a^{li}R^{k}_{1,t,l,\varepsilon}\Big)-e^{v_{i}(r)}R_{1,t,i,\varepsilon}^{k}=O(\varepsilon^{-\alpha}O_{m^{*}}(1+r)^{\tau-2})

(2.61)

and

\displaystyle-\Big(\frac{d^{2}}{dr^{2}}+\frac{1}{r}\frac{d}{dr}-\frac{k^{2}}{r^{2}}\Big)\Big(\sum_{l=1}^{n}a^{li}R^{k}_{2,t,l,\varepsilon}\Big)-e^{v_{i}(r)}R_{2,t,i,\varepsilon}^{k}=O(\varepsilon^{-\alpha}O_{m^{*}}(1+r)^{\tau-2}).

(2.62)

Notice that the right hand sides of (
2.61
) and of (
2.62
) are projections of
g_{i,\varepsilon}
on nodes higher than
1
.

Then, the function

\displaystyle\widetilde{w}^{II}_{t,i,\varepsilon}(x):=\sum_{k\geq 2}\Big(\sin(k\theta)\cdot R_{1,t,i,\varepsilon}^{k}(r)+\cos(k\theta)\cdot R_{2,t,i,\varepsilon}^{k}(r)\Big)\mbox{ in }B_{\frac{4\delta_{t}}{\varepsilon_{t}}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})

(2.63)

satisfies that

\displaystyle\widetilde{L}_{t,i,\varepsilon}\widetilde{w}^{II}_{t,\varepsilon}=\left\{\begin{aligned} &\varepsilon_{t}^{2}\widetilde{g}_{t,i,\varepsilon}^{II}&\mbox{ for }y\in\cup_{s=1}^{N}B_{\frac{5\delta_{s}}{\varepsilon_{s}}}(p_{s,\varepsilon});\\
&0&\mbox{ for }\Omega\backslash\cup_{s=1}^{N}B_{\frac{5\delta_{s}}{\varepsilon_{s}}}(p_{s,\varepsilon}).\end{aligned}\right.

(2.64)

Here,
\widetilde{g}_{t,i,\varepsilon}^{II}
is the projection of
\widetilde{g}_{t,i,\varepsilon}
over nodes higher than
1
in
\cup_{s=1}^{N}B_{\frac{5\delta_{s}}{\varepsilon_{s}}}(p_{s,\varepsilon})
. By a similar computation as in Proposition
2.12
and in Subsection
2.5
, we get
\|\widetilde{g}_{t,i,\varepsilon}^{II}\|_{\mathbb{Y}_{\varepsilon}}=\varepsilon^{-\alpha}O_{m^{*}}
. Denoting
w^{II}_{i,\varepsilon}(x)=\widetilde{w}^{II}_{t,\varepsilon}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})
. Notice that
(w^{II}_{1,\varepsilon},\cdots,w^{II}_{n,\varepsilon})\in E_{\varepsilon}
due to its frequency. By Theorem

A.1
, we get

\displaystyle\|w^{II}_{t,\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}=\varepsilon^{-\alpha-\tau}O_{m^{*}}.

(2.65)

Here,
\alpha
and
\tau
are two small positive constants. Then, we get

\displaystyle-\Big(\frac{d^{2}}{dr^{2}}+\frac{1}{r}\frac{d}{dr}-\frac{k^{2}}{r^{2}}\Big)R^{k}_{1,t,i,\varepsilon}=O(\varepsilon^{-\alpha-\tau}O_{m^{*}}(1+r)^{\tau-2}).

An elementary ODE method (see
[
31
, pp.2610-2616]
) implies that

|R_{1,i,\varepsilon}^{k}(r)|\leq C(\varepsilon r)^{k}+\frac{C\varepsilon^{m^{*}-2-\alpha-\tau}}{k^{2}}r^{2}(1+r)^{\tau-2}.

A similar computation can be given for
R^{k}_{2,t,i,\varepsilon}
. Then, we get for any
i=1,\cdots,n
and any
t=1,\cdots,N
,

|w^{II}_{i,\varepsilon}(x-p_{t,\varepsilon})|\leq C\varepsilon^{2}|x-p_{t,\varepsilon}|^{2}
for

x\in B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})
.

2.6.2
The 1-Frequency Part of
w_{\varepsilon}

Now we study the 1-frequency part of
w_{\varepsilon}
.
To begin with, we define the function

\displaystyle w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}:=w_{i,\varepsilon}-w^{0}_{i,\varepsilon}-w_{i,\varepsilon}^{II}.

By the above deduction,

(1)

w^{I}_{\varepsilon}:=(w^{I}_{1,\varepsilon},\cdots,w^{I}_{n,\varepsilon})\in E_{\varepsilon}
;

(2)

It holds that

\displaystyle\|w^{I}_{i,\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\leq\left\{\begin{aligned} &C\varepsilon^{m^{*}-2-\frac{\alpha}{2}}(\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon})^{2}&\mbox{ if }m^{*}\leq 3;\\
&C\varepsilon\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon}&\mbox{ if }m^{*}_{i}>3.\end{aligned}\right.

Assertion
(1)
holds because that
w_{\varepsilon}^{0},w_{\varepsilon}^{II},w_{\varepsilon}\in E_{\varepsilon}
. Notice that
w_{\varepsilon}\in E_{\varepsilon}
due to Proposition
2.12
and
w_{\varepsilon}^{II}\in E_{\varepsilon}
due to its frequency. For
w_{\varepsilon}^{0}
, we get

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{\Omega}\chi_{t}\partial_{x_{1}}v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})w^{0}_{i,\varepsilon}dx=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{\Omega}\chi_{t}\partial_{x_{2}}v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})w^{0}_{i,\varepsilon}dx=0

due to its symmetry and

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{N}\int_{\Omega}\chi_{t}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}w_{i}dx=\sum_{t=1}^{N}\int_{B_{5\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}w_{i}dx

\displaystyle=2\pi\sum_{t=1}^{N}\int_{0}^{5\delta_{t}}dr\int_{\partial B_{r}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}w_{i}dx=2\pi\sum_{t=1}^{N}\int_{0}^{5\delta_{t}}dr\int_{\partial B_{r}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}w_{i,\varepsilon}^{0}dx.

And
\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{\Omega}\sum_{s=1}^{N}\chi_{s}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}Z_{3,t,i,\varepsilon}^{*}w_{\varepsilon,i}^{0}dx=0
for the same reason.
Assertion
(2)
holds due to Proposition
2.13
, (
2.37
) and (
2.65
).

Moreover, due to the local expansion of
w^{0}_{\varepsilon}
and of
w_{\varepsilon}^{II}
, we know that for any
t=1,\cdots,N

\displaystyle w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}\big(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}}\big)=\sin\theta\cdot R_{1,t,i,\varepsilon}^{1}(r)+\cos\theta\cdot{R_{2,t,i,\varepsilon}^{1}(r)}

(2.66)

with

\displaystyle-\Big(\frac{d^{2}}{dr^{2}}+\frac{1}{r}\frac{d}{dr}-\frac{1}{r^{2}}\Big)\Big(\sum_{j=1}^{n}a^{ji}{R^{1}_{l,t,j,\varepsilon}}\Big)-e^{v_{i}(r)}R_{l,t,i,\varepsilon}^{1}

\displaystyle=\left\{\begin{aligned} &\frac{\varepsilon_{t}^{2}}{2\pi}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\widetilde{g}_{t,i,\varepsilon}(r\cos\theta,r\sin\theta)\cos\theta d\theta+\sum_{j=1}^{2}\sum_{t=1}^{N}\frac{r_{j,t}}{2\pi}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\widetilde{Z}_{j,t,i,\varepsilon}^{*}(r\cos\theta,r\sin\theta)\widetilde{\chi}_{t}e^{v_{i}(r\cos\theta,r\sin\theta)}\cos\theta d\theta\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\mbox{ if }l=1;\\
&\frac{\varepsilon_{t}^{2}}{2\pi}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\widetilde{g}_{t,i,\varepsilon}(r\cos\theta,r\sin\theta)\sin\theta d\theta+\sum_{j=1}^{2}\sum_{t=1}^{N}\frac{r_{j,t}}{2\pi}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\widetilde{Z}_{j,t,i,\varepsilon}^{*}(r\cos\theta,r\sin\theta)\widetilde{\chi}_{t}e^{v_{i}(r\cos\theta,r\sin\theta)}\sin\theta d\theta\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\mbox{ if }l=2.\end{aligned}\right.

By a similar method as in
[
31
, pp.2610]
, we find a sequence
\vec{C}_{\varepsilon}\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
bounded in
\varepsilon
and

w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}(x)\sim\varepsilon_{t}\vec{C}_{\varepsilon}\cdot(x-p_{t,\varepsilon})
as
x\to p_{t,\varepsilon}
.

2.6.3
Conclusion: A Refined Lyapunov-Schmidt Reduction

In this subsection, we provide a refined version of Proposition
2.12
based on Fourier analysis.

Proposition 2.15

Under Hypotheses (

H
1

), (

H
2

) and (

H
3

), there exists a constant
\varepsilon_{0}>0
and for any
\varepsilon\in(0,\varepsilon_{0})
, there exists a
w_{\varepsilon}=(w_{\varepsilon}^{1},\cdots,w_{\varepsilon}^{n})\in E_{\varepsilon}
satisfies

\displaystyle Q_{\varepsilon}(L_{1,\varepsilon}(w_{\varepsilon})-g_{1,\varepsilon}(x,w_{\varepsilon}),\cdots,L_{n,\varepsilon}(w_{\varepsilon})-g_{n,\varepsilon}(x,w_{\varepsilon}))=0.

Moreover, there exists three
C^{1}
maps
w_{\varepsilon}^{0}
,
w_{\varepsilon}^{I}
,
w_{\varepsilon}^{II}
of
\varepsilon
in
E_{\varepsilon}
with
w_{\varepsilon}=w_{\varepsilon}^{0}+w_{\varepsilon}^{I}+w_{\varepsilon}^{II}
. Here,
w_{\varepsilon}^{0}
satisfies

(W^{0}_{1})

\|w^{0}_{i,\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}=O_{m^{*}}\varepsilon^{-\alpha-\tau}
;

(W^{0}_{2})

w^{0}_{i,\varepsilon}|_{B_{2\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}=R^{0}_{t,i,\varepsilon}(|x-p_{t,\varepsilon}|)
for any
t=1,\cdots,N
. Here,
R_{t,i,\varepsilon}^{0}
are radial functions satisfying (
2.35
);

(W^{0}_{3})

w^{0}_{i,\varepsilon}|_{\Omega\backslash\cup_{t=1}^{N}B_{3\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}=0
;

(W^{0}_{4})

\Big|w^{0}_{i,\varepsilon}\big(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}}\big)\Big|\leq\varepsilon^{-\alpha}O_{m^{*}}\big(1+\Big|\frac{y-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}}\Big|\big)^{\tau}
for
x\in B_{3\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})
and for any
t=1,\cdots,N
.

w_{\varepsilon}^{I}
satisfies

(W^{I}_{1})

It holds that

\displaystyle\|w^{I}_{i,\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}\leq\left\{\begin{aligned} &C\varepsilon^{m^{*}-2-\frac{\alpha}{2}}(\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon})^{2}&\mbox{ if }m^{*}\leq 3;\\
&C\varepsilon\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon}&\mbox{ if }m^{*}_{i}>3.\end{aligned}\right.

(W^{I}_{2})

for any
t=1,\cdots,N
,
w^{I}_{i,\varepsilon}
has an expansion in the form of (
2.66
) in
B_{2\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})
;

(W^{I}_{3})

there exists a sequence
\vec{C}_{\varepsilon}\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
bounded in
\varepsilon
and

w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}(x)\sim\varepsilon_{t}\vec{C}_{\varepsilon}\cdot(x-p_{t,\varepsilon})
as
x\to p_{t,\varepsilon}
.

And
w_{\varepsilon}^{II}
satisfies

(W^{II}_{1})

\|w^{II}_{i,\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}=O_{m^{*}}\varepsilon^{-\alpha-\tau}
;

(W^{II}_{2})

w^{II}_{i,\varepsilon}|_{B_{2\delta}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}=\widetilde{w}^{II}_{i,\varepsilon}(x-p_{t,\varepsilon})
for any
t=1,\cdots,N
and
\widetilde{w}^{II}_{i,\varepsilon}
satisfies (
2.63
);

(W^{II}_{3})

for any
i=1,\cdots,n
and any
t=1,\cdots,N
,

|w^{II}_{i,\varepsilon}(x-p_{t,\varepsilon})|\leq C\varepsilon^{2}|x-p_{t,\varepsilon}|^{2}
for

x\in B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})
.

Here,
\alpha
and
\tau
are generically small positive numbers.

3
Finite-dimensional Problems and Proof of Theorem
1.1

In this section, we complete the proof by analyzing the reduced finite-dimensional problem with a standard approach used in
[
16
,
22
]
. The central issue arises when the projection of
S(U_{1,\varepsilon}+w_{1,\varepsilon},\cdots,U_{n,\varepsilon}+w_{n,\varepsilon})
onto
F_{\varepsilon}
(see (
2.17
)) vanishes. Consequently, the primary focus is on the terms involving the projections of
S(U_{1,\varepsilon}+w_{1,\varepsilon}^{I},\cdots,U_{n,\varepsilon}+w_{n,\varepsilon}^{I})
.

In Subsections
3.2
,
3.3
, and
3.4
, we explore the projections onto
Z^{*}_{j,t,\varepsilon}
for
j=1,2,3
and
t=1,\cdots,N
. Importantly, the projections onto
e_{i}=(0,\cdots,1,\cdots,0)
for
i=1,\cdots,n
clearly vanish due to the double periodicity of the arguments. Based on the computations presented above, we complete the proof of Theorem
1.1
in Subsection
3.5
.

3.1
A Finite-dimensional Problem

Denoting

\displaystyle L_{\varepsilon}w_{\varepsilon}=(L_{1,\varepsilon}w_{\varepsilon},\cdots,L_{n,\varepsilon}w_{\varepsilon})

and

\displaystyle g_{\varepsilon}(x,w_{\varepsilon})=(g_{1,\varepsilon}(x,w_{\varepsilon}),\cdots,g_{n,\varepsilon}(x,w_{\varepsilon})),

the reduction scheme ensures the existence of
w_{\varepsilon}=(w_{1,\varepsilon},\cdots,w_{n,\varepsilon})
such that

\displaystyle L_{\varepsilon}w_{\varepsilon}-g_{\varepsilon}(x,w_{\varepsilon})=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{2}\sum_{t=1}^{N}r_{j,t}\Big(\chi_{t}\partial_{x_{j}}v_{1}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})\sum_{s=1}^{N}e^{v_{1}(\frac{x-p_{s,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{s}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{s}}},\cdots,

\displaystyle\quad\quad\chi_{t}\partial_{x_{j}}v_{n}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}}}{\varepsilon_{t}})\sum_{s=1}^{N}e^{v_{n}(\frac{x-p_{s,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{s}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{s}}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\sum_{t=1}^{N}r_{3,t}\Big(Z_{3,t,1,\varepsilon}^{*}\sum_{s=1}^{N}\chi_{s}e^{v_{1}(\frac{x-p_{s,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{s}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{s}}},\cdots,Z_{3,t,n,\varepsilon}^{*}\sum_{s=1}^{N}\chi_{s}e^{v_{n}(\frac{x-p_{s,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{s}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{s}}}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big(s_{1}\sum_{t=1}^{N}\chi_{s}e^{v_{1}(\frac{x-p_{s,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{s}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{s}}},\cdots,s_{n}\sum_{s=1}^{N}\chi_{t}e^{v_{n}(\frac{x-p_{s,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{s}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{s}}}\Big)

with

\displaystyle L_{i,\varepsilon}w_{\varepsilon}-g_{i,\varepsilon}(x,w_{\varepsilon})=-\Delta(U_{\varepsilon}^{i}+w_{\varepsilon}^{i})-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\Bigg(\frac{h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}}}-1\Bigg)

(3.1)

for
i=1,\cdots,n
and for some constant
r_{1,1},\cdots,r_{1,N},r_{2,1},\cdots,r_{2,N},r_{3,1},\cdots,r_{3,N},s_{1},\cdots,s_{n}\in\mathbb{R}
. The following lemma provides a condition to find a solution to Problem (
1.1
).

Lemma 3.1

If

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{\Omega}\Big[L_{i,\varepsilon}w_{\varepsilon}-g_{i,\varepsilon}(x,w_{\varepsilon})\Big]Z_{j,t,i,\varepsilon}^{*}=0

(3.2)

for
t=1,\cdots,N
and
j=1,2,3
, then
r_{1,1}=\cdots=r_{1,N}=r_{2,1}=\cdots=r_{2,N}=r_{3,1}=\cdots=r_{3,N}=s_{1}=\cdots=s_{n}=0
.

Proof.

For
j^{\prime}=1,2
and
t^{\prime}=1,\cdots,N
, multiplying (
3.1
) by
Z_{j^{\prime},t^{\prime},i,\varepsilon}^{*}
, integrating over
\Omega
and summing with respect to
i
, we get

\displaystyle 0=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{\Omega}\Big[L_{i,\varepsilon}w_{\varepsilon}-g_{i,\varepsilon}(x,w_{\varepsilon})\Big]Z_{j,i,\varepsilon}^{*}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{3}r_{j,t}\int_{\Omega}\sum_{s=1}^{N}\chi_{s}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{s,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{s}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{s}}}Z_{j,t,i,\varepsilon}^{*}Z_{j^{\prime},t^{\prime},i,\varepsilon}^{*}+s_{i}\int_{\Omega}\chi_{t}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{s}}}Z_{j^{\prime},t^{\prime},i,\varepsilon}\Bigg).

By the symmetry, we get
r_{1,1}=\cdots=r_{1,N}=r_{1,i}=r_{2,N}=0
.

Now we study
r_{3,1},\cdots,r_{3,N},s_{1},\cdots,s_{n}
. To do this, notice that

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\Big(\sum_{t=1}^{N}r_{3,t}\sum_{s=1}^{N}\chi_{s}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{s,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{s}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{s}}}Z_{3,t,i,\varepsilon}^{*}+s_{i}\sum_{s=1}^{N}\chi_{s}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{s,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{s}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{s}}}\Big)=0.

(3.3)

This is due to (
3.1
) and the fact that
U_{\varepsilon}^{i}+w_{\varepsilon}^{i}
are double periodic.
On the other hand, multiplying
Z_{3,i,\varepsilon}^{*}
on both sides of (
3.1
), integrating over
\Omega
and summing with respect to
i
, we get

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Bigg(\sum_{t=1}^{N}r_{3,t}\int_{\Omega}\sum_{s=1}^{N}\chi_{s}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{s}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{s}}}Z_{3,t,i,\varepsilon}^{*}Z_{3,t^{\prime},i,\varepsilon}^{*}+s_{i}\int_{\Omega}\sum_{s=1}^{N}\chi_{s}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{s,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{s}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{s}}}Z_{3,t^{\prime},i,\varepsilon}^{*}\Bigg)=0.

(3.4)

By a direct computation, we get for
t=t^{\prime}

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{\Omega}\sum_{s=1}^{N}\chi_{s}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{s,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{s}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{s}}}Z_{3,t,i,\varepsilon}^{*}Z_{3,t^{\prime},i,\varepsilon}^{*}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}

\displaystyle\Big[(N-1)(m_{i}^{*}-2)^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{v_{i}}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{v_{i}}|x\cdot\nabla v_{i}+2|^{2}+o_{\varepsilon}(1)\Big]\varepsilon_{t}^{2},

for
t\neq t^{\prime}

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{\Omega}\sum_{s=1}^{N}\chi_{s}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{s,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{s}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{s}}}Z_{3,t,i,\varepsilon}^{*}Z_{3,t^{\prime},i,\varepsilon}^{*}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}

\displaystyle\Big[(N-2)(m_{i}^{*}-2)^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{v_{i}}+o_{\varepsilon}(1)\Big]\varepsilon_{t}\varepsilon_{t^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\sum_{s=1}^{N}\chi_{s}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{s,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{s}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{s}}}Z_{3,t^{\prime},i,\varepsilon}^{*}=\Big[(N-1)(2-m_{i}^{*})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{v_{i}}+o_{\varepsilon}(1)\Big]

and

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\sum_{s=1}^{N}\chi_{s}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{s,\varepsilon_{s}}}{\varepsilon_{s}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{s}}}=N\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{v_{i}}.

Now we rewrite (
3.3
) and (
3.4
) into a linear systems. To do this, we need to introduce several vectors and matrices.
We denote

\displaystyle X=(\varepsilon_{1}r_{3,1},\cdots,\varepsilon_{N}r_{3N},s_{1},\cdots,s_{n})^{T}.

Define

G_{1}
to be the
N\times N
martix such that

\displaystyle(i,i)\mbox{-element of }G_{1}=(N-1)\sum_{i=1}^{n}(m_{i}^{*}-2)^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{v_{i}}dx+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{v_{i}}(\nabla v_{i}\cdot x+2)^{2}dx

and

\displaystyle(i,j)\mbox{-element of }G_{1}=(N-2)\sum_{i=1}^{n}(m_{i}^{*}-2)^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{v_{i}}dx.

for
i\neq j
.
Define the
n\times N
matrix

G_{3}=\begin{pmatrix}(N-1)(2-m_{1}^{*})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{v_{1}}&\cdots&(N-1)(2-m_{1}^{*})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{v_{1}}\\
\vdots&\cdots&\vdots\\
(N-1)(2-m_{n}^{*})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{v_{n}}&\cdots&(N-1)(2-m_{n}^{*})\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{v_{n}}\\
\end{pmatrix}.

Define the
n\times n
diagonal matrix

\displaystyle G_{2}=\mbox{diag}\Big(N\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{v_{1}},\cdots,N\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{v_{n}}\Big),

which is invertible.
Then, the above gives us

\begin{pmatrix}G_{1}+o_{\varepsilon}(1)&G_{3}^{T}+o_{\varepsilon}(1)\\
G_{3}+o_{\varepsilon}(1)&G_{2}+o_{\varepsilon}(1)\end{pmatrix}X=0.

Now we are in the position to prove the invertibility of the matrix

\begin{pmatrix}G_{1}&G_{3}^{T}\\
G_{3}&G_{2}\end{pmatrix}.

To do this, we only need to check the invertibility of
G_{1}-G_{3}^{T}G_{2}^{-1}G_{3}
. By a direct computation, all of the elements of
G_{3}^{T}G_{2}^{-1}G_{3}
are equal to

\displaystyle\frac{(N-1)^{2}}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(m_{i}^{*}-2)^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{v_{i}}.

Then, we get

\displaystyle(i,i)\mbox{-element of }G_{1}-G_{3}^{T}G_{2}^{-1}G_{3}

\displaystyle=\Big(N-1-\frac{(N-1)^{2}}{N}\Big)\sum_{i=1}^{n}(m_{i}^{*}-2)^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{v_{i}}dx

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{v_{i}}(\nabla v_{i}\cdot x+2)^{2}dx

and

\displaystyle(i,j)\mbox{-element of }G_{1}-G_{3}^{T}G_{2}^{-1}G_{3}=\Big(N-2-\frac{(N-1)^{2}}{N}\Big)\sum_{i=1}^{n}(m_{i}^{*}-2)^{2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}e^{v_{i}}dx.

for
i\neq j
.
This is evidently invertible, which implies that

r_{3,1}=\cdots=r_{3,N}=s_{1}=\cdots=s_{n}=0
.
\Box

Lemma 3.2

It holds that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{\Omega}\Big[L_{i,\varepsilon}w_{\varepsilon}-g_{i,\varepsilon}(x,w_{\varepsilon})\Big]Z_{j,t,i,\varepsilon}^{*}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{\Omega}\Bigg[-\Delta\Big(U_{\varepsilon}^{i}+\sum_{l=1}^{n}a^{il}w^{I}_{i,\varepsilon}\Big)-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\Bigg(\frac{h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w^{I}_{i,\varepsilon}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}e^{U_{i},\varepsilon+w^{I}_{i,\varepsilon}}}-1\Bigg)\Bigg]Z_{i,j,\varepsilon}^{*}

\displaystyle\quad+R_{j,t,\varepsilon}

with

R_{j,t,\varepsilon}=o_{\varepsilon}(1)\varepsilon O_{m^{*}}

for any
t=1,\cdots,N
and any
j=1,2,3
. Here,
w^{I}_{i,\varepsilon}
is the
i
-th component of the 1-frequency part of
w_{\varepsilon}
defined in Subsection
2.6.2
.

Proof.

We only consider the cases for
j=1,2
and the case
j=3
is similar. Since
w_{i,\varepsilon}=w_{i,\varepsilon}^{0}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{II}
(see Proposition
2.15
) for any
i=1,\cdots,n
, we get

\displaystyle L_{i,\varepsilon}w_{\varepsilon}-g_{i,\varepsilon}(x,w_{\varepsilon})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle L_{i,\varepsilon}(w_{\varepsilon}^{0}+w_{\varepsilon}^{II})-\Delta(U^{i}_{\varepsilon}+\sum_{l=1}^{n}a^{il}w_{l,\varepsilon}^{I})+\rho_{i,\varepsilon}-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\frac{h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\Bigg\{\Big(K_{i,\varepsilon}(x)-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\frac{h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}\Big)(w_{i,\varepsilon}^{0}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{II})\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad-\Bigg\{\frac{K_{i,\varepsilon}(x)}{\rho_{i}^{*}}\int_{\Omega}K_{i,\varepsilon}(x)(w_{i,\varepsilon}^{0}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{II})-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\frac{h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{(\int_{\Omega}h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}})^{2}}\times

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\times\int_{\Omega}h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}(w_{i,\varepsilon}^{0}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{II})\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad-\Bigg\{\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\frac{h_{i}(x)e^{\ U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}}}-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\frac{h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\Bigg(\frac{h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}\Bigg)(w^{0}_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{II})

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\frac{\rho_{i,\varepsilon}h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\Big(\int_{\Omega}h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}\Big)^{2}}\int_{\Omega}\Big(h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}(w^{0}_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{II})\Big)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=:L_{i,\varepsilon}(w_{\varepsilon}^{0}+w_{\varepsilon}^{II})-\Delta(U^{i}_{\varepsilon}+\sum_{l=1}^{n}a^{il}w_{l,\varepsilon}^{I})+\rho_{i,\varepsilon}-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\frac{h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}

\displaystyle\quad+A^{\prime}_{2,i}-A^{\prime}_{3,i}-N^{\prime}_{i,\varepsilon}.

(3.5)

Then, for any
t=1,\cdots,N
and any
j=1,2,3
, we get

\displaystyle R_{j,t,\varepsilon}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{\Omega}\Big(L_{i,\varepsilon}(w_{\varepsilon}^{0}+w_{\varepsilon}^{II})+A^{\prime}_{2,i}-A^{\prime}_{3,i}-N^{\prime}_{i,\varepsilon}\Big)Z_{i,j,\varepsilon}^{*}.

(3.6)

Here,

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}L_{i,\varepsilon}(w_{\varepsilon}^{0}+w_{\varepsilon}^{II})Z_{i,j,\varepsilon}^{*}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}L_{i,\varepsilon}Z_{i,j,\varepsilon}^{*}(w_{\varepsilon}^{0}+w_{\varepsilon}^{II})=0

and

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{\Omega}A^{\prime}_{2,i}Z_{i,j,\varepsilon}^{*}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Bigg\{\Big(K_{i,\varepsilon}(x)-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\frac{h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}\Big)(w_{i,\varepsilon}^{0}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{II})\Bigg\}Z_{i,j,\varepsilon}^{*}

\displaystyle=O(1)\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{t=1}^{N}\int_{B_{5\delta}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}\Bigg\{1-\frac{h_{i}(x)}{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}e^{2\pi m_{i}^{*}({G^{*}}(x;p_{t,\varepsilon})-{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon}))}\Bigg\}\times

\displaystyle\quad\times(w_{i,\varepsilon}^{0}+w^{II}_{i,\varepsilon})Z_{i,j,\varepsilon}^{*}dx+O\Big[\|w^{I}_{\varepsilon}\|_{\infty}(\|w_{\varepsilon}^{0}\|_{\infty}+\|w_{\varepsilon}^{II}\|_{\infty})\Big]\cdot O(\varepsilon)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{t=1}^{N}\int_{B_{5\delta}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}O(|x|^{2})(w_{i,\varepsilon}^{0}+w^{II}_{i,\varepsilon})Z_{i,j,\varepsilon}^{*}dx+O\Big[\|w^{I}_{\varepsilon}\|_{\infty}(\|w_{\varepsilon}^{0}\|_{\infty}+\|w_{\varepsilon}^{II}\|_{\infty})\Big]\cdot O(\varepsilon)

\displaystyle=o_{\varepsilon}(1)\varepsilon O_{m^{*}}.

Moreover, as in the proof of Proposition
2.12
, we get

\displaystyle A^{\prime}_{i,3}(x)=O_{m^{*}}(\|w_{\varepsilon}^{0}\|_{\infty}+\|w_{\varepsilon}^{II}\|_{\infty})e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}

in
B_{5\delta}(p_{t,\varepsilon})
. This gives that

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}A^{\prime}_{i,3}Z_{i,j,\varepsilon}^{*}=o_{\varepsilon}(1)\varepsilon O_{m^{*}}.

For
N^{\prime}_{i,\varepsilon}
, it holds that

\displaystyle N^{\prime}_{i,\varepsilon}\leq Ce^{U_{i,\varepsilon}}(\|w_{\varepsilon}^{0}\|_{\infty}+\|w_{\varepsilon}^{II}\|_{\infty})^{2}.

Then, we get

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}N^{\prime}_{i,\varepsilon}Z_{i,j,\varepsilon}^{*}=o_{\varepsilon}(1)\varepsilon O_{m^{*}}.

Together, they proves the result.
\Box

3.2
Projections of
S(U_{1,\varepsilon}+w_{1,\varepsilon}^{I},\cdots,U_{n,\varepsilon}+w_{n,\varepsilon}^{I})
on
Z_{1,t,\varepsilon}^{*}(x)
and on
Z_{2,t,\varepsilon}^{*}(x)

In this subsection, we compute the projection of

S(U_{1,\varepsilon}+w_{1,\varepsilon}^{I},\cdots,U_{n,\varepsilon}+w_{n,\varepsilon}^{I})

on
Z_{1,t,\varepsilon}^{*}(x)
and on
Z_{2,t,\varepsilon}^{*}(x)
. Here,
w^{I}_{i,\varepsilon}
is the
i
-th component of the 1-frequency part of
w_{\varepsilon}
defined in Subsection
2.6.2
.

Proposition 3.3

It holds that for any
t=1,\cdots,N
, we get

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{\Omega}

\displaystyle\Bigg[-\Delta\Big(U_{\varepsilon}^{i}(x)+\sum_{l=1}^{n}a^{il}w_{l,\varepsilon}^{I}\Big)-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\Bigg(\frac{h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}-1\Bigg)\Bigg]Z_{1,t,i,\varepsilon}^{*}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Big[\partial_{x_{1}}\ln h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})+2\pi m_{i}^{*}\partial_{x_{1}}{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{s,\varepsilon})\Big]\rho_{i}^{*}\varepsilon_{t}+\varepsilon O_{m^{*}}.

and

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{\Omega}

\displaystyle\Bigg[-\Delta\Big(U_{\varepsilon}^{i}(x)+\sum_{l=1}^{n}a^{il}w_{l,\varepsilon}^{I}\Big)-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\Bigg(\frac{h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}-1\Bigg)\Bigg]Z_{2,t,i,\varepsilon}^{*}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Big[\partial_{x_{2}}\ln h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})+2\pi m_{i}^{*}\partial_{x_{2}}{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{s,\varepsilon})\Big]\rho_{i}^{*}\varepsilon_{t}+\varepsilon O_{m^{*}}

Here, as it is defined in (
1.4
),

\displaystyle O_{m^{*}}=\left\{\begin{aligned} &O(\varepsilon^{m^{*}-2})&\mbox{ if }m^{*}<4;\\
&O(\varepsilon^{2}\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon})&\mbox{ if }m^{*}=4.\end{aligned}\right.

Proof.

We only prove the first identity since the second is similar.
By the symmetry, we get

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{\Omega}

\displaystyle\Bigg[-\Delta\Big(U_{\varepsilon}^{i}(x)+\sum_{l=1}^{n}a^{il}w_{l,\varepsilon}^{I}\Big)-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\Bigg(\frac{h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}-1\Bigg)\Bigg]Z_{1,t,i,\varepsilon}^{*}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{\Omega}\Bigg[-\Delta\Big(U_{\varepsilon}^{i}(x)+\sum_{l=1}^{n}a^{il}w_{l,\varepsilon}^{I}\Big)-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\frac{h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}\Bigg]Z_{1,t,i,\varepsilon}^{*}

\displaystyle=\int_{\Omega\backslash B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}+\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Bigg[-\Delta\Big(U_{\varepsilon}^{i}(x)+\sum_{l=1}^{n}a^{il}w_{l,\varepsilon}^{I}\Big)-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\frac{h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}\Bigg]Z_{1,t,i,\varepsilon}^{*}

\displaystyle=:I_{1}+I_{2}.

We compute
I_{1}
and
I_{2}
separately.

By the symmetry and the fact that
w^{I}_{\varepsilon}\in E_{\varepsilon}
, we get for
m^{*}\leq 4

\displaystyle I_{1}=\varepsilon O_{m^{*}}.

(3.7)

On the other hand, if
m^{*}<4
,
first we estimate the integral
\int_{\Omega}h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}
.
By a similar method as in Lemma
2.9
, we get

\displaystyle h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w^{I}_{i,\varepsilon}}

\displaystyle=h_{i}(x)e^{\sum_{s=1}^{N}W_{i,s,\varepsilon_{s}}(x)+w^{I}_{i,\varepsilon}}e^{\sum_{s\neq t}v_{i}\big(\frac{\delta_{s}}{\varepsilon_{s}}\big)+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{s}}}e^{\sum_{s\neq t}2\pi m_{i}^{*}[G(x,p_{s,\varepsilon})+\frac{1}{2\pi}\ln\delta_{s}-\gamma(p_{t,\varepsilon},p_{s,\varepsilon})]}

\displaystyle=e^{v_{i}\big(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}}\big)+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}\Big(\Pi_{s\neq t}\varepsilon_{s}\Big)^{m_{i}^{*}-2}e^{2\pi m_{i}^{*}\sum_{s\neq t}[G(p_{t,\varepsilon},p_{s,\varepsilon})-\gamma(p_{s,\varepsilon},p_{s,\varepsilon})]}\times

\displaystyle\quad\times\Big[h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})+\nabla_{y}\Big(h_{i}(y)e^{2\pi m^{*}[{G^{*}}(y;p_{t,\varepsilon})-{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon})]}\Big)\Big|_{y=p_{t,\varepsilon}}\cdot(x-p_{t,\varepsilon})+O(|x-p_{t,\varepsilon}|)^{2}\Big]\times

\displaystyle\quad\times[1+|(1-\chi_{t})(W_{j,t,\varepsilon}^{**}-W_{j,t,\varepsilon}^{*})|+O(|(1-\chi_{t})(W_{j,t,\varepsilon}^{**}-W_{j,t,\varepsilon}^{*})|^{2})]

\displaystyle\quad\times[1+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}+O(|w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}|^{2})].

By the symmetry of
w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}
and Proposition
2.15
, we get for any
t=1,\cdots,N

\displaystyle\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w^{I}_{i,\varepsilon}}dx=\frac{\rho_{i}^{*}h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}{N}e^{\sum_{l\neq t}2\pi m_{i}^{*}[G(p_{t,\varepsilon},p_{l,\varepsilon})-\gamma(p_{l,\varepsilon},p_{l,\varepsilon})]}\Big(\Pi_{l\neq s}\varepsilon_{l}\Big)^{m_{i}^{*}-2}(1+O_{m^{*}}).

Then, due to the symmetry, we get

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\rho_{i,\varepsilon}}{\rho_{i}^{*}}\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}e^{v_{i}\Big(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}}\Big)+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}\Big[e^{\ln\frac{h_{i}(x)}{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}+2\pi m_{i}^{*}[{G^{*}}(x;p_{t,\varepsilon})-{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon})]+w^{I}_{i,\varepsilon}+(1-\chi_{t})(W_{j,t,\varepsilon}^{**}-W_{j,t,\varepsilon}^{*})}-1\Big]\times

\displaystyle\quad\times\partial_{x_{1}}v_{i}\Big(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}}}{\varepsilon_{t}}\Big)dx\times(1+O_{m^{*}})

\displaystyle=-\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}e^{v_{i}\Big(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}}\Big)+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}\Big[\Big(\frac{\nabla h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}+2\pi m_{i}^{*}\nabla_{y}{G^{*}}(y;p_{t,\varepsilon})|_{y=p_{t,\varepsilon}}\Big)\cdot(x-p_{t,\varepsilon})

\displaystyle\quad+(x-p_{t,\varepsilon})D(x-p_{t,\varepsilon})^{T}+O(|x-p_{t,\varepsilon}|^{3})+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}+(1-\chi_{t})(W_{j,t,\varepsilon}^{**}-W_{j,t,\varepsilon}^{*})+O(|w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}|^{2})

\displaystyle\quad+O(|(1-\chi_{t})(W_{j,t,\varepsilon}^{**}-W_{j,t,\varepsilon}^{*})|^{2})\Big]\partial_{x_{1}}v_{i}\Big(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}}\Big)dx\cdot(1+o_{\varepsilon}(1))

\displaystyle=-\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{B_{\delta}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}e^{v_{i}\Big(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}}\Big)+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}\Big[\Big(\frac{\partial_{x_{1}}h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}+2\pi m_{i}^{*}\partial_{x_{1}}{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon})\Big)(x-p_{t,\varepsilon})_{1}

\displaystyle\quad+O(|x-p_{t,\varepsilon}|^{3})\Big]\partial_{x_{1}}v_{i}\Big(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}}\Big)dx\cdot(1+o_{\varepsilon}(1))

\displaystyle\quad+o_{\varepsilon}(1)\varepsilon O_{m^{*}}.

Here,
(x-p_{t,\varepsilon})_{1}
is the first component of
x-p_{t,\varepsilon}
.
In the last step, we use the symmetry and the fact that
w_{\varepsilon}^{I}\in E_{\varepsilon}
.
The matrix
D
is defined as

D=D^{2}\exp\Big\{\ln\frac{h_{i}(x)}{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}})}+2\pi m_{i}^{*}[{G^{*}}(x;p_{t,\varepsilon})-{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon})]+(1-\chi_{t})(W_{j,t,\varepsilon}^{**}-W_{j,t,\varepsilon}^{*})\Big\}\Big|_{p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}}}.

By a direct computation, we get

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(0)}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}|x|^{3}\Big|\partial_{x_{1}}v_{i}\Big(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}}\Big)\Big|dx\leq C\varepsilon^{3}_{t}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{B_{\frac{\delta_{t}}{\varepsilon_{t}}}(0)}e^{v_{i}(y)}|y|^{3}dy=C\varepsilon_{t}^{m^{*}-1}

and

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}})}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}(x-p_{t,\varepsilon})_{1}\partial_{x_{1}}v_{i}\Big(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}}\Big)dx=\varepsilon_{t}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{B_{\frac{\delta}{\varepsilon_{t}}}(0)}e^{v_{i}(x)}x_{1}\partial_{x_{1}}v_{i}(x)dx.

Notice that

\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{1}}\big(x_{1}e^{v_{i}(x)}\big)=e^{v_{i}(x)}+x_{1}\partial_{x_{1}}v_{i}(x)e^{v_{i}(x)}
.
We get

\int_{\partial B_{\frac{\delta_{t}}{\varepsilon_{t}}}(0)}x_{1}e^{v_{i}(x)}dS=\int_{B_{\frac{\delta_{t}}{\varepsilon_{t}}}(0)}e^{v_{i}(x)}dx+\int_{B_{\frac{\delta_{t}}{\varepsilon_{t}}}(0)}x_{1}\partial_{x_{1}}v_{i}(x)e^{v_{i}(x)}dx
.
This implies that

\displaystyle-\int_{B_{\frac{\delta_{t}}{\varepsilon_{t}}}(0)}x_{1}\partial_{x_{1}}v_{i}(x)e^{v_{i}(x)}dx=\frac{\rho_{i}^{*}}{N}+O(\varepsilon^{m^{*}-2}).

Then, we get for
m^{*}<4

\displaystyle I_{2}=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Big[\partial_{x_{1}}\ln h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})+2\pi m_{i}^{*}\partial_{x_{1}}{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon},p_{s,\varepsilon})\Big]\rho_{i}^{*}\varepsilon_{t}+O(\varepsilon^{m^{*}-1}).

(3.8)

If
m^{*}=4
for
i=1,\cdots,n
, notice that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(0)}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon+t}}|x|^{3}\Big|\partial_{x_{1}}v_{i}\Big(\frac{x}{\varepsilon_{t}}\Big)\Big|dx

\displaystyle\leq C\varepsilon^{3}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{B_{\frac{\delta_{t}}{\varepsilon_{t}}(0)}}e^{v_{i}(x)}|x|^{2}dx

\displaystyle=C\varepsilon^{3}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Bigg\{\int_{B_{\frac{\delta_{t}}{\varepsilon_{t}}(0)}\backslash B_{\sqrt{\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}}(0)}+\int_{B_{\sqrt{\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}}(0)}\Bigg\}e^{v_{i}(x)}|x|^{2}dx

\displaystyle=O\Big(\varepsilon^{3}\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Big)+C\varepsilon^{3}\int_{1}^{\frac{\delta_{t}}{\varepsilon}}\frac{dr}{r}=O\Big(\varepsilon^{3}\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Big).

By a similar approach, it holds that

\displaystyle I_{2}=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Big[\partial_{x_{1}}\ln h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})+2\pi m_{i}^{*}\partial_{x_{1}}{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon})\Big]\rho_{i}^{*}\varepsilon_{t}+O\Big(\varepsilon^{3}\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Big).

(3.9)

Combining (
3.7
), (
3.8
) and (
3.9
), we get

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{\Omega}

\displaystyle\Bigg[-\Delta\Big(U_{\varepsilon}^{i}(x)+\sum_{l=1}^{n}a^{il}w_{l,\varepsilon}^{I}\Big)-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\Bigg(\frac{h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}-1\Bigg)\Bigg]Z_{1,t,i,\varepsilon}^{*}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{N}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Big[\partial_{x_{1}}\ln h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})+2\pi m_{i}^{*}\partial_{x_{1}}{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon})\Big]\rho_{i}^{*}\varepsilon_{t}+\varepsilon O_{m^{*}}.

\Box

3.3
Projection of
S(U_{1,\varepsilon}+w_{1,\varepsilon}^{I},\cdots,U_{n,\varepsilon}+w_{n,\varepsilon}^{I})
on
Z_{3,t,\varepsilon}^{*}(x)
:
m^{*}<4
Case

In this subsection, we compute the projection of

S(U_{1,\varepsilon}+w_{1,\varepsilon}^{I},\cdots,U_{n,\varepsilon}+w_{n,\varepsilon}^{I})

on

\mbox{span}\{Z_{3,t,\varepsilon}^{*}(x)\}
with
m^{*}<4
.
Here,
w^{I}_{i,\varepsilon}
is the
i
-th component of the 1-frequency part of
w_{\varepsilon}
defined in Subsection
2.6.2
.

Lemma 3.4

Assume that
m^{*}<4
, for any
t=1,\cdots,N
, we get

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{\Omega}\Bigg[-\Delta(U_{\varepsilon}^{i}+\sum_{l=1}^{n}a^{il}w_{l,\varepsilon}^{I})-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\Big(\frac{h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}-1\Big)\Bigg]Z_{3,i,t,\varepsilon}^{*}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in\hat{I}}\frac{m^{*}-2}{N}\Big(D_{i,t}+\sum_{s\neq t}\frac{e^{{H_{i,s}(p^{*})}}}{e^{{H_{i,t}(p^{*})}}}D_{i,s}+o_{\delta}(1)\Big)\varepsilon_{t}^{m^{*}-1}+2\pi\Lambda_{I,N}(\rho_{\varepsilon})\varepsilon_{t}+o_{\varepsilon}(1)\varepsilon^{m^{*}-1}.

Here, as it is defined in (
1.8
),

D_{i,s}:=\Bigg[\int_{\Omega_{t}}\frac{\frac{h_{i}(x)}{h_{i}(p_{s}^{*})}e^{2\pi m^{*}({G^{*}}(x;p_{s,\varepsilon})-{G^{*}}(p_{s}^{*};p_{s}^{*}))}-1}{|x-p_{s}^{*}|^{m^{*}}}dx-\int_{\Omega_{s}^{c}}\frac{dx}{|x-p_{s}^{*}|^{m^{*}}}\Bigg]e^{I_{i}}

for
i\in\hat{I}=\{i\in I|m^{*}_{i}=m^{*}\}
and
s=1,\cdots,N
. The sub-domains
\Omega_{s}\subset\Omega
satisfy

•

\overline{\cup_{s=1}^{N}\Omega_{s}}=\overline{\Omega}
;

•

\Omega_{s}\cap\Omega_{s^{\prime}}=\emptyset
for any
s,s^{\prime}=1,\cdots,N
with
s\neq s^{\prime}
;

•

B_{3\delta_{s}}(p_{s,\varepsilon_{s}})\in\Omega_{s}
for any
s=1,\cdots,N
.

Proof.

Consider the integration
for any
t=1,\cdots,N
, we get

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\Bigg[-\Delta(U_{\varepsilon}^{i}+\sum_{l=1}^{n}a^{il}w_{l,\varepsilon}^{I})-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\Big(\frac{h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}-1\Big)\Bigg]Z_{3,i,t,\varepsilon}^{*}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{\Omega}\Bigg[-\Delta U_{\varepsilon}^{i}-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\Big(\frac{h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}-1\Big)\Bigg]Z_{3,i,t,\varepsilon}^{*}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{\Omega}\Bigg[-\Delta U_{\varepsilon}^{i}-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\Big(\frac{h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}-1\Big)\Bigg][Z_{3,i,t,\varepsilon}^{*}(x)-(2-m_{i}^{*})\varepsilon_{t}]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}})}\Bigg[-\Delta U_{\varepsilon}^{i}-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\Big(\frac{h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}-1\Big)\Bigg][Z_{3,i,t,\varepsilon}^{*}(x)-(2-m_{i}^{*})\varepsilon_{t}].

Here, the first equation is due to symmetry and the second to the fact that

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\Bigg[-\Delta U_{\varepsilon}^{i}-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\Big(\frac{h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}-1\Big)\Bigg]dx=0

while the third to that
\mbox{supp}\big(Z_{3,i,t,\varepsilon}^{*}(x)-(2-m_{i}^{*})\varepsilon_{t}\Big)\subset\overline{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}
.
Then,

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\Bigg[-\Delta U_{\varepsilon}^{i}-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\Big(\frac{h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}-1\Big)\Bigg]Z_{3,i,t,\varepsilon}^{*}

\displaystyle=\Bigg\{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{B_{3\delta}(p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}})}\Bigg[-\Delta U_{\varepsilon}^{i}-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\Big(\frac{h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}-1\Big)\Bigg]Z_{3,i,t,\varepsilon}^{*}(x)\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad-\Bigg\{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{B_{3\delta}(p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}})}\Bigg[-\Delta U_{\varepsilon}^{i}-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\Big(\frac{h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}-1\Big)\Bigg](2-m_{i}^{*})\varepsilon_{t}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=:II_{1}-II_{2}.

Now we estimate
II_{1}
and
II_{2}
separately. To do so, it is necessary to refine the estimate of
\int_{\Omega}h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}
.

Step 1. Refined estimate of
\int_{\Omega}h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}
.

For any
x\in B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})
, it holds that

\displaystyle\quad h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}

\displaystyle=h_{i}(x)e^{W_{i,t}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}+\sum_{s\neq t}W_{i,s}}

\displaystyle=h_{i}(x)e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}e^{\sum_{s\neq t}v_{i}(\frac{\delta_{s}}{\varepsilon_{s}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{s}}+m_{i}^{*}\ln\delta_{s}}e^{2\pi m_{i}^{*}({G^{*}}(x;p_{t,\varepsilon})-{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon}))}e^{(1-\chi_{t})(W^{**}_{i,t,\varepsilon}-W^{*}_{i,t,\varepsilon})}

\displaystyle=h_{i}(x)e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}\times e^{2\pi m_{i}^{*}({G^{*}}(x;p_{t,\varepsilon})-{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon}))}\times

\displaystyle\quad\times e^{2\pi m_{i}^{*}\sum_{s\neq t}[G(p_{t,\varepsilon},p_{s,\varepsilon})-\gamma(p_{s,\varepsilon},p_{s,\varepsilon})]}e^{(1-\chi_{t})(W^{**}_{i,t,\varepsilon}-W^{*}_{i,t,\varepsilon})}e^{NI_{i}}\Big[\Pi_{s\neq t}\varepsilon_{s}^{m_{i}^{*}-2}\Big(1+\sum_{j=1}^{n}a_{ij}e^{I_{j}}\big(\frac{\varepsilon_{s}}{\delta_{s}}\big)^{m_{j}^{*}-2}+O_{m^{*}}^{2}\Big)\Big].

Integrating over
B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})
, we get

\displaystyle\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}

\displaystyle h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}dx=e^{2\pi m_{i}^{*}\sum_{s\neq t}[G(p_{t,\varepsilon},p_{s,\varepsilon})-\gamma(p_{s,\varepsilon},p_{s,\varepsilon})]}e^{NI_{i}}\times

\displaystyle\times\Big[\Pi_{s\neq t}\varepsilon_{s}^{m_{i}^{*}-2}\Big(1+\sum_{j=1}^{n}a_{ij}e^{I_{j}}\big(\frac{\varepsilon}{\delta_{s}}\big)^{m_{j}^{*}-2}+O_{m^{*}}^{2}\Big)\Big]\times

\displaystyle\times\int_{B_{\delta}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}h_{i}(x)e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}e^{2\pi m_{i}^{*}({G^{*}}(x;p_{t,\varepsilon})-{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon}))+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}+(1-\chi_{t})(W^{**}_{i,t,\varepsilon}-W^{*}_{i,t,\varepsilon})}dx.

Here,

\displaystyle\int_{B_{\delta}(p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}})}h_{i}(x)e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}e^{2\pi m_{i}^{*}({G^{*}}(x;p_{t,\varepsilon})-{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon}))}e^{w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}+(1-\chi_{t})(W^{**}_{i,t,\varepsilon}-W^{*}_{i,t,\varepsilon})}dx

\displaystyle=h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}+h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}\times

\displaystyle\times\Big\{\frac{h_{i}(x)}{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}e^{2\pi m_{i}^{*}({G^{*}}(x;p_{t,\varepsilon})-{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon}))+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}+(1-\chi_{t})(W^{**}_{i,t,\varepsilon}-W^{*}_{i,t,\varepsilon})}-1\Big\}+O_{m^{*}}^{2}

\displaystyle=h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})\frac{\rho_{i}^{*}}{N}+2\pi(2-m_{i}^{*})\big(\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{\delta_{t}}\big)^{m_{i}^{*}-2}h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})

\displaystyle+e^{I_{i}}\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}\frac{\frac{h_{i}(x)}{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}e^{2\pi m_{i}^{*}({G^{*}}(x;p_{t,\varepsilon})-{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon}))+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}+(1-\chi_{t})(W^{**}_{i,t,\varepsilon}-W^{*}_{i,t,\varepsilon})}-1}{|x-p_{t,\varepsilon}|^{m_{i}^{*}}}dx\cdot\varepsilon_{t}^{m_{i}^{*}-2}+O_{m^{*}}^{2}.

Here, the integral converges due to Assertion
(W^{I}_{3})
of Proposition
2.15
.
Therefore, we get

\displaystyle\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}dx=e^{2\pi m_{i}^{*}\sum_{s\neq t}[G(p_{t,\varepsilon},p_{s,\varepsilon})-\gamma(p_{s,\varepsilon},p_{s,\varepsilon})]}e^{NI_{i}}\times

\displaystyle\times\Big[\Pi_{s\neq t}\varepsilon_{s}^{m_{i}^{*}-2}\Big(1+\sum_{j=1}^{n}a_{ij}e^{I_{j}}\big(\frac{\varepsilon_{s}}{\delta_{s}}\big)^{m_{j}^{*}-2}+O_{m^{*}}^{2}\Big)\Big]\times

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})\frac{\rho_{i}^{*}}{N}+2\pi(2-m_{i}^{*})\big(\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{\delta_{t}}\big)^{m_{i}^{*}-2}h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})

\displaystyle+e^{I_{i}}\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}\frac{\frac{h_{i}(x)}{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}e^{2\pi m_{i}^{*}({G^{*}}(x;p_{t,\varepsilon})-{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon}))+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}+(1-\chi_{t})(W^{**}_{i,t,\varepsilon}-W^{*}_{i,t,\varepsilon})}-1}{|x-p_{t,\varepsilon}|^{m_{i}^{*}}}dx\cdot\varepsilon_{t}^{m^{*}-2}+O_{m^{*}}^{2}\Bigg\}.

(3.10)

On the other hand, for any
x\in\Omega\backslash\cup_{s=1}^{N}B_{\delta_{s}}(p_{s,\varepsilon})
, we get

\displaystyle h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}=h_{i}(x)e^{\sum_{s=1}^{N}v_{i}(\frac{\delta_{s}}{\varepsilon_{s}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{s}}+m_{i}^{*}\ln\delta}e^{2\pi m_{i}^{*}\sum_{s=1}^{N}[G(x,p_{s,\varepsilon})-\gamma(p_{s,\varepsilon},p_{s,\varepsilon})]+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}.

Then, for any
t=1,\cdots,N
and any
x\in B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})
,

\displaystyle h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}

\displaystyle=e^{NI_{i}}\Big[\Pi_{s=1}^{N}\varepsilon_{s}^{m_{i}^{*}-2}\Big(1+\sum_{j=1}^{n}a_{ij}e^{I_{j}}\big(\frac{\varepsilon_{s}}{\delta}\big)^{m_{j}^{*}-2}+O_{m^{*}}^{2}\Big)\Big]e^{2\pi m_{i}^{*}\sum_{s\neq t}[G(p_{t,\varepsilon},p_{s,\varepsilon})-\gamma(p_{s,\varepsilon},p_{s,\varepsilon})]}\times

\displaystyle\quad\times\frac{e^{2\pi m_{i}^{*}({G^{*}}(x;p_{t,\varepsilon})-{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon}))+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}+(1-\chi_{t})(W^{**}_{i,t,\varepsilon}-W^{*}_{i,t,\varepsilon})}}{|x-p_{t,\varepsilon}|^{m_{i}^{*}}}.

Select
N
sub-domains
\Omega_{t}\subset\Omega
such that

•

\overline{\cup_{s=1}^{N}\Omega_{s}}=\overline{\Omega}
;

•

\Omega_{s}\cap\Omega_{s^{\prime}}=\emptyset
for any
s,s^{\prime}=1,\cdots,N
with
s\neq s^{\prime}
;

•

B_{3\delta_{s}}(p_{s,\varepsilon_{s}})\in\Omega_{s}
for any
s=1,\cdots,N
.

Then, we get

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}dx

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1}^{N}\Big[\Pi_{s\neq t}\varepsilon_{s}^{m_{i}^{*}-2}\Big(1+\sum_{j=1}^{n}a_{ij}\big(\frac{\varepsilon_{s}}{\delta_{s}}\big)^{m_{j}^{*}-2}+O_{m^{*}}^{2}\Big)\Big]\times e^{\sum_{s\neq t}2\pi m_{i}^{*}[G(p_{t,\varepsilon},p_{s,\varepsilon})-\gamma(p_{s,\varepsilon},p_{s,\varepsilon})]}\times

\displaystyle\times\Bigg\{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})\frac{\rho_{i}^{*}}{N}+\Bigg[e^{I_{i}}\int_{\Omega_{t}}\frac{h_{i}(x)e^{2\pi m_{i}^{*}({G^{*}}(x;p_{t,\varepsilon})-{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon}))+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}+(1-\chi_{t})(W^{**}_{i,t,\varepsilon}-W^{*}_{i,t,\varepsilon})}-h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}{|x-p_{t,\varepsilon}|^{m_{i}^{*}}}dx

\displaystyle-e^{I_{i}}\int_{\Omega_{t}^{c}}\frac{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})dx}{|x-p_{t,\varepsilon}|^{m_{i}^{*}}}\Bigg]\varepsilon_{t}^{m_{i}^{*}-2}+O_{m^{*}}^{2}\Bigg\}.

(3.11)

In the following, for the sake of simplicity, we denote

\displaystyle D^{\varepsilon}_{i,s}:=

\displaystyle\Bigg[\int_{\Omega_{t}}\frac{\frac{h_{i}(x)}{h_{i}(p_{s,\varepsilon})}e^{2\pi m_{i}^{*}({G^{*}}(x;p_{s,\varepsilon})-{G^{*}}(p_{s,\varepsilon};p_{s,\varepsilon}))+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}+(1-\chi_{s})(W^{**}_{i,s,\varepsilon}-W^{*}_{i,s,\varepsilon})}-1}{|x-p_{s,\varepsilon}|^{m_{i}^{*}}}dx-\int_{\Omega_{s}^{c}}\frac{dx}{|x-p_{s,\varepsilon}|^{m_{i}^{*}}}\Bigg]e^{I_{i}}.

Based on the above, we have the following claims.

Claim 3.5

For any
t=1,\cdots,N
and any
x\in B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}})
, we get

\displaystyle\frac{h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}=\frac{h_{i}(x)e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}+(1-\chi_{t})(W_{i,t,\varepsilon}^{**}-W_{i,t,\varepsilon}^{**})}}{\mbox{DENOMINATOR}}.

Here,

DENOMINATOR

\displaystyle=h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}})\frac{\rho_{i}^{*}}{N}+h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})D^{\varepsilon}_{i,t}\varepsilon_{t}^{m_{i}^{*}-2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{s\neq t}\big(\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{\varepsilon_{s}}\big)^{m_{i}^{*}-2}\Big(1+\sum_{j=1}^{n}a_{ij}\Big[(\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{\delta_{t}})^{m_{j}^{*}-2}-(\frac{\varepsilon_{s}}{\delta_{s}})^{m_{j}^{*}-2}\Big]+O_{m^{*}}^{2}\Big)\times

\displaystyle\times e^{{H_{i,s}(p_{\varepsilon})-H_{i,t}(p_{\varepsilon})}}{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}})}\Big(\frac{\rho_{i}^{*}}{N}+D^{\varepsilon}_{i,s}\varepsilon_{s}^{m_{i}^{*}-2}\Big)+O_{m^{*}}^{2}.

Under Hypothesis (

H
2

), we get

\displaystyle(\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{\delta_{t}})^{m_{j}^{*}-2}-(\frac{\varepsilon_{s}}{\delta_{s}})^{m_{j}^{*}-2}=O_{m^{*}}^{2}\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon}.

Claim 3.6

Under Hypothesis (

H
3

), i.e.

\displaystyle(\varepsilon_{t}/\varepsilon_{s})^{m_{i}^{*}-2}=e^{{H_{i,t}(p_{\varepsilon})-H_{i,s}(p_{\varepsilon})}}+o_{\delta}(1)\varepsilon^{m^{*}-2},

we get

\displaystyle DENOMINATOR=

\displaystyle h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})\rho_{i}^{*}+\big(h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})D_{i,t}^{\varepsilon}+\sum_{s\neq t}e^{{H_{i,t}(p_{\varepsilon})-H_{i,s}(p_{\varepsilon})}}{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}D_{i,s}^{\varepsilon}

\displaystyle\quad+o_{\delta}(1)\big)\varepsilon_{t}^{m^{*}_{i}-2}+o_{\varepsilon}(1)\varepsilon O_{m^{*}}.

(3.12)

Step 2. Estimating
II_{1}
and
II_{2}
.

By a direct computation, we get

\displaystyle II_{1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}\Bigg[-\Delta U_{\varepsilon}^{i}-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\Big(\frac{h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}-1\Big)\Bigg]Z_{3,i,t,\varepsilon}^{*}(x)dx

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}\Bigg[-\Delta U_{\varepsilon}^{i}-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\Big(\frac{h_{i}(x)e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}+(1-\chi_{t})(W^{**}_{i,t,\varepsilon}-W^{*}_{i,t,\varepsilon})}}{DENOMINATOR}-1\Big)\Bigg]Z_{3,i,t,\varepsilon}^{*}(x)dx

Here, we use Claim
3.5
and
DENOMINATOR
is defined as in (
3.12
). Then, we get

\displaystyle II_{1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}\Bigg[-\Delta U_{\varepsilon}^{i}-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\Big(\frac{h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}-1\Big)\Bigg]Z_{3,i,t,\varepsilon}^{*}(x)dx

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}\Big[1-\frac{\rho_{i,\varepsilon}h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}{\mbox{DENOMINATOR}}\Big]e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})}Z_{3,t,i,\varepsilon}^{*}(x)+O(\varepsilon^{2m^{*}-3})

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Big[\rho_{i,\varepsilon}-2\pi\sum_{l=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{n}a^{ij}m_{j}^{*}\Big]\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}Z_{3,t,i,\varepsilon}^{*}(x)dx

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\rho_{i,\varepsilon}h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}{\mbox{DENOMINATOR}}\int_{B_{3\delta}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}\Big[1

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{h_{i}(x)}{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}e^{2\pi m_{i}^{*}({G^{*}}(x;p_{t,\varepsilon})-{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon}))+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}+(1-\chi_{t})(W_{i,t,\varepsilon}^{**}-W_{i,t,\varepsilon}^{*})}\Big]Z_{3,i,t,\varepsilon}^{*}(x)dx

\displaystyle=o_{\varepsilon}(1)\varepsilon^{m^{*}-1}+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Big[\rho_{i,\varepsilon}-2\pi\sum_{l=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{n}a^{ij}m_{j}^{*}\Big]\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}[(2-m_{i}^{*})\varepsilon_{t}]dx

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\rho_{i,\varepsilon}h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}{\mbox{DENOMINATOR}}\int_{B_{3\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}\Big[1

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{h_{i}(x)}{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}e^{2\pi m_{i}^{*}({G^{*}}(x;p_{t,\varepsilon})-{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon}))+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}+(1-\chi_{t})(W_{i,t,\varepsilon}^{**}-W_{i,t,\varepsilon}^{*})}\Big](2-m_{i}^{*})\varepsilon_{t}dx.

(3.13)

By a similar method, we get

\displaystyle II_{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}\Bigg[-\Delta(U_{\varepsilon}^{i}+\sum_{l=1}^{n}a^{il}w_{l,\varepsilon}^{I})-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\Big(\frac{h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}-1\Big)\Bigg][(2-m_{i}^{*})\varepsilon_{t}]dx

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}\Big[1-\frac{\rho_{i,\varepsilon}h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}{\mbox{DENOMINATOR}}\Big]e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})}[(2-m_{i}^{*})\varepsilon_{t}]

\displaystyle+o_{\varepsilon}(1)\varepsilon^{m^{*}-1}+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Big[\rho_{i,\varepsilon}-2\pi\sum_{l=1}^{N}\sum_{j=1}^{n}a^{ij}m_{j}^{*}\Big]\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}[(2-m_{i}^{*})\varepsilon_{t}]dx

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\rho_{i,\varepsilon}h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}{\mbox{DENOMINATOR}}\int_{B_{3\delta}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}\Big[1

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{h_{i}(x)}{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}e^{2\pi m_{i}^{*}({G^{*}}(x;p_{t,\varepsilon})-{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon}))+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}+(1-\chi_{t})(W_{i,t,\varepsilon}^{**}-W_{i,t,\varepsilon}^{*})}\Big]\times(2-m_{i}^{*})\varepsilon_{t}dx.

(3.14)

Combining (
3.13
) and (
3.14
), we get

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\Bigg[-\Delta(U_{\varepsilon}^{i}+\sum_{l=1}^{n}a^{il}w_{l,\varepsilon}^{I})-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\Big(\frac{h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}-1\Big)\Bigg]Z_{3,i,t,\varepsilon}^{*}

\displaystyle=II_{1}-II_{2}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}\Big[1-\frac{\rho_{i,\varepsilon}h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}{\mbox{DENOMINATOR}}\Big]e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})}[(2-m_{i}^{*})\varepsilon_{t}]dx+o_{\varepsilon}(1)\varepsilon^{m^{*}-1}.

Step 3. Completing the proof.

By a direct computation, we get

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\Bigg[-\Delta(U_{\varepsilon}^{i}+\sum_{l=1}^{n}a^{il}w_{l,\varepsilon}^{I})-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\Big(\frac{h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}-1\Big)\Bigg]Z_{3,i,t,\varepsilon}^{*}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}\Big[1-\frac{\rho_{i,\varepsilon}h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}{\mbox{DENOMINATOR}}\Big]e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})}[(2-m_{i}^{*})\varepsilon_{t}]dx+o_{\varepsilon}(1)\varepsilon^{m_{i}^{*}-1}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{i=1}^{n}(2-m_{i}^{*})\varepsilon_{t}\Big[\frac{\rho_{i}^{*}}{N}-2\pi e^{I_{i}}\big(\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{\delta_{t}}\big)^{m_{i}^{*}-2}\Big]\cdot\Big[1-\frac{\rho_{i,\varepsilon}h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}{\mbox{DENOMINATOR}}\Big]+o_{\varepsilon}(1)\varepsilon^{m^{*}-1}

\displaystyle=\Bigg\{-\sum_{i=1}^{n}(2-m_{i}^{*})\varepsilon_{t}\Big[\frac{\rho_{i}^{*}}{N}-2\pi e^{I_{i}}\big(\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{\delta_{t}}\big)^{m_{i}^{*}-2}\Big]\cdot\frac{\rho_{i,\varepsilon}}{\rho_{i}^{*}}\Big[1-\frac{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}})}{\rho_{i}^{*}\cdot\mbox{DENOMINATOR}}\Big]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad+\Bigg\{-\sum_{i=1}^{n}(2-m_{i}^{*})\varepsilon_{t}\Big[\frac{\rho_{i}^{*}}{N}-2\pi e^{I_{i}}\big(\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{\delta_{t}}\big)^{m_{i}^{*}-2}\Big]\cdot\frac{\rho_{i}^{*}-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}}{\rho_{i}^{*}}\Bigg\}+o_{\varepsilon}(1)\varepsilon^{m_{i}^{*}-1}

\displaystyle=:II^{*}_{1}+II^{*}_{2}+o_{\varepsilon}(1)\varepsilon^{m_{i}^{*}-1}.

Here,
DENOMINATOR
is defined as in (
3.12
).
Now we compute
II^{*}_{1}
and
II^{*}_{2}
separately. First, for
II_{2}^{*}
, we get

\displaystyle II_{2}^{*}=2\pi\Lambda_{I,N}(\rho_{\varepsilon})\varepsilon_{t}+o_{\varepsilon}(1)\varepsilon^{m^{*}-1}.

(3.15)

This is due to Lemma
2.7
.
Now we study
II^{*}_{1}
.
Notice that for any infinitesimal
o
, we have

\displaystyle 1-\frac{1}{1+o}=o+O(o^{2}).

By Claim
3.6
, we get

\displaystyle 1-\frac{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}{\rho_{i}^{*}\cdot\mbox{DENOMINATOR}}

\displaystyle=(\rho_{i}^{*})^{-1}\Big(D_{i,t}^{\varepsilon}+\sum_{s\neq t}{\frac{e^{(m_{i}^{*}-2)H_{i,s}(p_{\varepsilon})}}{e^{(m^{*}_{i}-2)H_{i,t}(p_{\varepsilon})}}}D_{i,s}^{\varepsilon}+o_{\delta}(1)\Big)\varepsilon_{t}^{m^{*}_{i}-2}+o_{\varepsilon}(1)O_{m^{*}}.

Then, we get

\displaystyle II^{*}_{1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in\hat{I}}\frac{m_{i}^{*}-2}{N}\Big(D_{i,t}^{\varepsilon}+\sum_{s\neq t}{\frac{e^{H_{i,s}(p_{\varepsilon})}}{e^{H_{i,t}(p_{\varepsilon})}}}D_{i,s}^{\varepsilon}+o_{\delta}(1)\Big)\varepsilon_{t}^{m^{*}-2}+o_{\varepsilon}(1)\varepsilon O_{m^{*}}.

(3.16)

Here,
\hat{I}=\{i\in I|m^{*}_{i}=m^{*}\}
.
Combining (
3.15
) and (
3.16
), we get

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\Bigg[-\Delta(U_{\varepsilon}^{i}+\sum_{l=1}^{n}a^{il}w_{l,\varepsilon}^{I})-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\Big(\frac{h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}-1\Big)\Bigg]Z_{3,i,t,\varepsilon}^{*}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in\hat{I}}\frac{m^{*}-2}{N}\Big(D_{i,t}^{\varepsilon}+\sum_{s\neq t}{\frac{e^{H_{i,s}(p_{\varepsilon})}}{e^{H_{i,t}(p_{\varepsilon})}}}D_{i,s}^{\varepsilon}+o_{\delta}(1)\Big)\varepsilon_{t}^{m^{*}-1}+2\pi\Lambda_{I,N}(\rho_{\varepsilon})\varepsilon_{t}+o_{\varepsilon}(1)\varepsilon^{m^{*}-1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\in\hat{I}}\frac{m^{*}-2}{N}\Big(D_{i,t}+\sum_{s\neq t}{\frac{e^{H_{i,s}(p^{*})}}{e^{H_{i,t}(p^{*})}}}D_{i,s}+o_{\delta}(1)\Big)\varepsilon_{t}^{m^{*}-1}+2\pi\Lambda_{I,N}(\rho_{\varepsilon})\varepsilon_{t}+o_{\varepsilon}(1)\varepsilon^{m^{*}-1}.

Here,

D^{\varepsilon}_{i,s}:=\Bigg[\int_{\Omega_{s}}\frac{\frac{h_{i}(x)}{h_{i}(p_{s,\varepsilon})}e^{2\pi m_{i}^{*}({G^{*}}(x;p_{s,\varepsilon})-{G^{*}}(p_{s,\varepsilon};p_{s,\varepsilon}))}-1}{|x-p_{s,\varepsilon}|^{m_{i}^{*}}}dx-\int_{\Omega_{s}^{c}}\frac{dx}{|x-p_{s,\varepsilon}|^{m_{i}^{*}}}\Bigg]e^{I_{i}}

and

D_{i,s}:=\Bigg[\int_{\Omega_{t}}\frac{\frac{h_{i}(x)}{h_{i}(p_{s}^{*})}e^{2\pi m_{i}^{*}({G^{*}}(x;p_{s}^{*})-{G^{*}}(p_{s}^{*};p_{s}^{*}))}-1}{|x-p_{s}^{*}|^{m_{i}^{*}}}dx-\int_{\Omega_{s}^{c}}\frac{dx}{|x-p_{s}^{*}|^{m_{i}^{*}}}\Bigg]e^{I_{i}}

for
i\in\hat{I}
and
s=1,\cdots,N
.
This proves the result.

\Box

3.4
Projection of
S(U_{1,\varepsilon}+w_{1,\varepsilon}^{I},\cdots,U_{n,\varepsilon}+w_{n,\varepsilon}^{I})
on
Z_{3,t,\varepsilon}^{*}(x)
:
m^{*}=4
Case

In this subsection, we compute the projection of

S(U_{1,\varepsilon}+w_{1,\varepsilon}^{I},\cdots,U_{n,\varepsilon}+w_{n,\varepsilon}^{I})

on

\mbox{span}\{Z_{3,t,\varepsilon}^{*}(x)\}
with
m^{*}=4
.
Here,
w^{I}_{i,\varepsilon}
is the
i
-th component of the 1-frequency part of
w_{\varepsilon}
defined in Subsection
2.6.2
.

Lemma 3.7

Assume that
m^{*}=4
and that

\sum_{i=1}^{n}e^{I_{i}}\sum_{t=1}^{N}e^{2H_{i,t}(p^{*})}h_{i}(p_{t}^{*})L_{i,t}

is non-zero.
Then,
we get

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\Bigg[-\Delta(U_{\varepsilon}^{i}+\sum_{l=1}^{n}a^{il}w_{l,\varepsilon}^{I})-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\Big(\frac{h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}-1\Big)\Bigg]Z_{3,i,t,\varepsilon}^{*}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{2}{N}\Big(L_{i,t}+\sum_{s\neq t}{\frac{e^{H_{i,s}(p^{*})}}{e^{H_{i,t}(p^{*})}}}L_{i,s}+o_{\delta}(1)\Big)\varepsilon_{t}^{3}\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}+2\pi\Lambda_{I,N}(\rho_{\varepsilon})\varepsilon_{t}+o_{\varepsilon}(1)\varepsilon_{t}^{3}\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}.

Here,

\displaystyle L_{i,s}=\Bigg|\frac{\nabla h_{i}(p_{s}^{*})}{h_{i}(p_{s}^{*})}+8\pi\nabla_{1}{G^{*}}(p_{s}^{*};p_{s}^{*})\Bigg|^{2}e^{I_{i}}+\Bigg(\frac{\Delta h_{i}(p_{s}^{*})}{h_{i}(p_{s}^{*})}+8N\pi\Bigg)e^{I_{i}}

for
i=1,\cdots,n
and
s=1,\cdots,N
as defined in (
1.6
).

Proof.

Since a lot of the computations are similar to Lemma
3.4
, we only sketch the proof.

Step 1. Refined estimate on
\int_{\Omega}h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}
.

To begin with, notice that

\displaystyle\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}})}

\displaystyle h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}dx=e^{8\pi\sum_{s\neq t}[G(p_{t,\varepsilon},p_{s,\varepsilon})-\gamma(p_{s,\varepsilon},p_{s,\varepsilon})]}e^{NI_{i}}\times

\displaystyle\times\Big[\Pi_{s\neq t}\varepsilon_{s}^{2}\Big(1+\sum_{j=1}^{n}a_{ij}e^{I_{j}}\big(\frac{\varepsilon_{s}}{\delta_{s}}\big)^{2}+O_{m^{*}}^{2}\Big)\Big]\int_{B_{\delta}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}h_{i}(x)e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}\times

\displaystyle\times e^{8\pi({G^{*}}(x;p_{t,\varepsilon})-{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon}))+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}+(1-\chi_{t})(W^{**}_{i,t,\varepsilon}-W^{*}_{i,t,\varepsilon})}dx.

Here,

\displaystyle\int_{B_{\delta}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}

\displaystyle h_{i}(x)e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}e^{8\pi({G^{*}}(x;p_{t,\varepsilon})-{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon}))}e^{w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}+(1-\chi_{t})(W^{**}_{i,t,\varepsilon}-W^{*}_{i,t,\varepsilon})}dx

\displaystyle=h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})\int_{B_{\delta}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}+h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})\int_{B_{\delta}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}\times

\displaystyle\times\Big\{\frac{h_{i}(x)}{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}})}e^{8\pi({G^{*}}(x;p_{t,\varepsilon})-{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon}))+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}+(1-\chi_{t})(W^{**}_{i,t,\varepsilon}-W^{*}_{i,t,\varepsilon})}-1\Big\}+O_{m^{*}}^{2}

\displaystyle=h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}})\frac{\rho_{i}^{*}}{N}-4\pi\big(\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{\delta_{t}}\big)^{2}h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}})

\displaystyle+\int_{B_{\delta}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}\Bigg\{\frac{h_{i}(x)}{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}e^{8\pi({G^{*}}(x;p_{t,\varepsilon})-{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon}))+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}+(1-\chi_{t})(W^{**}_{i,t,\varepsilon}-W^{*}_{i,t,\varepsilon})}-1\Big\}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}dx+O_{m^{*}}^{2}.

By the symmetry and the obvious identity

\displaystyle\Delta(e^{f})=(\Delta f)e^{f}+|\nabla f|^{2}e^{f},

we get

\displaystyle\int_{B_{\delta}(p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}})}\Bigg\{\frac{h_{i}(x)}{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}})}e^{8\pi({G^{*}}(x;p_{t,\varepsilon})-{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon}))+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}+(1-\chi_{t})(W^{**}_{i,t,\varepsilon}-W^{*}_{i,t,\varepsilon})}-1\Big\}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}dx

\displaystyle=\frac{e^{-I_{i}}}{2}\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}\Big(L^{\varepsilon}_{i,t}|x|^{2}+O(|x|^{4})\Big)e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}dx

\displaystyle=\frac{e^{-I_{i}}}{2}\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}L^{\varepsilon}_{i,t}|x|^{2}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}dx+O(\varepsilon^{2})

with

\displaystyle L^{\varepsilon}_{i,t}=\Bigg|\frac{\nabla h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}+8\pi\nabla_{1}{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon})+\nabla w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}(p_{t,\varepsilon})\Bigg|^{2}e^{I_{i}}+\Bigg(\frac{\Delta h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}+8N\pi\Bigg)e^{I_{i}}.

By a direct computation, we get

\displaystyle\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(0)}|x|^{2}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}dx

\displaystyle=\varepsilon_{t}^{2}\int_{B_{\frac{\delta_{t}}{\varepsilon_{t}}}(0)}|y|^{2}e^{v_{i}(y)}dy=\varepsilon_{t}^{2}\Big(C+2\pi e^{I_{i}}\int_{1}^{\frac{\delta_{t}}{\varepsilon_{t}}}\frac{dr}{r}\Big)

\displaystyle=2\pi e^{I_{i}}\varepsilon_{t}^{2}\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}+O(\varepsilon^{2}).

This implies that

\displaystyle\int_{B_{\delta}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}\Bigg\{\frac{h_{i}(x)}{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}e^{8\pi({G^{*}}(x;p_{t,\varepsilon})-{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon}))+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}+(1-\chi_{t})(W^{**}_{i,t,\varepsilon}-W^{*}_{i,t,\varepsilon})}-1\Big\}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon}}dx

\displaystyle=\pi L_{i,\varepsilon}^{*}\varepsilon_{t}^{2}\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}+O(\varepsilon^{2}).

Then, we get

\displaystyle\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}})}h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}dx

\displaystyle=e^{8\pi\sum_{s\neq t}[G(p_{t,\varepsilon},p_{s,\varepsilon})-\gamma(p_{s,\varepsilon},p_{s,\varepsilon})]}e^{NI_{i}}\times

\displaystyle\times\Big[\Pi_{s\neq t}\varepsilon_{s}^{2}\Big(1+\sum_{j=1}^{n}a_{ij}e^{I_{j}}\big(\frac{\varepsilon_{s}}{\delta_{s}}\big)^{2}+O_{m^{*}}^{2}\Big)\Big]\times

\displaystyle\times\Big\{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})\frac{\rho_{i}^{*}}{N}+\pi L_{i,\varepsilon}^{*}\varepsilon_{t}^{2}\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}+O(\varepsilon^{2})\Big\}

\displaystyle=e^{8\pi\sum_{s\neq t}[G(p_{t,\varepsilon},p_{s,\varepsilon})-\gamma(p_{s,\varepsilon},p_{s,\varepsilon})]}e^{NI_{i}}\times

\displaystyle\times\Pi_{s\neq t}\varepsilon_{s}^{2}\Big\{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})\frac{\rho_{i}^{*}}{N}+\pi L_{i,\varepsilon}^{*}\varepsilon_{t}^{2}\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}+O(\varepsilon^{2})\Big\}.

Lemma
2.9
implies that

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}

\displaystyle h_{i}(x)e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}dx=e^{8\pi\sum_{s\neq t}[G(p_{t,\varepsilon},p_{s,\varepsilon})-\gamma(p_{s,\varepsilon_{s}},p_{s,\varepsilon_{s}})]}e^{NI_{i}}\Pi_{s\neq t}\varepsilon_{s}^{2}\Big\{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})\frac{\rho_{i}^{*}}{N}+\pi L_{i,\varepsilon}^{*}(p_{\varepsilon})\varepsilon_{t}^{2}\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}+O(\varepsilon^{2})\Big\}.

(3.17)

Step 2. Proof of Lemma
3.7
.

First, notice that

\displaystyle\int_{B_{\delta}(0)}

\displaystyle e^{v_{i}\big(\frac{x}{\varepsilon_{t}}\big)+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}x_{1}^{2}\Big[|x|v^{\prime}_{i}\big(\frac{x}{\varepsilon_{t}}\big)+2\varepsilon_{t}\Big]dx

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\int_{B_{\delta}(0)}e^{v_{i}\big(\frac{x}{\varepsilon_{t}}\big)+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}|x|^{2}\Big[|x|v^{\prime}_{i}\big(\frac{x}{\varepsilon_{t}}\big)+2\varepsilon_{t}\Big]dx

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\int_{B_{\delta}(0)\backslash B_{\varepsilon_{t}(\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}})^{\frac{1}{100}}}(0)}+\int_{B_{\varepsilon_{t}(\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}})^{\frac{1}{100}}}(0)}e^{v_{i}\big(\frac{x}{\varepsilon_{t}}\big)+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}|x|^{2}\Big[|x|v^{\prime}_{i}\big(\frac{x}{\varepsilon_{t}}\big)+2\varepsilon_{t}\Big]dx

\displaystyle=-2\pi e^{I_{i}}\varepsilon_{t}^{3}\int_{(\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}})^{\frac{1}{100}}}^{\frac{\delta}{\varepsilon_{t}}}\frac{r^{3}dr}{r^{4}}+O\Big(\varepsilon_{t}^{-1}\int_{0}^{\varepsilon_{t}\big(\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}\big)^{\frac{1}{100}}}r^{3}dr\Big)

\displaystyle=-2\pi e^{I_{i}}\varepsilon_{t}^{3}\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}+O\Big(\varepsilon_{t}^{3}\ln\Big(\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}\Big)^{\frac{1}{100}}+\varepsilon_{t}^{3}\Big(\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}\Big)^{\frac{1}{25}}\Big)

\displaystyle=-2\pi e^{I_{i}}\varepsilon_{t}^{3}\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}+o_{\varepsilon}\Big(\varepsilon^{3}\Big(\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\Big)^{\frac{1}{4}}\Big).

Then, by a similar computation as in
Step 2
and
Step 3
the proof of Lemma
3.4
, we find

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\Bigg[-\Delta(U_{\varepsilon}^{i}+\sum_{l=1}^{n}a^{il}w_{l,\varepsilon}^{I})-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\Big(\frac{h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}-1\Big)\Bigg]Z_{3,i,t,\varepsilon}^{*}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Bigg\{2\sum_{i=1}^{n}\varepsilon_{t}\Big[\frac{\rho_{i}^{*}}{N}-2\pi e^{I_{i}}\big(\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{\delta_{t}}\big)^{2}\Big]\cdot\frac{\rho_{i,\varepsilon}}{\rho_{i}^{*}}\Big[1-\frac{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}})}{\rho_{i}^{*}\cdot\mbox{DENOMINATOR}}\Big]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad+\Bigg\{2\sum_{i=1}^{n}\varepsilon_{t}\Big[\frac{\rho_{i}^{*}}{N}-2\pi e^{I_{i}}\big(\frac{\varepsilon_{t}}{\delta_{t}}\big)^{2}\Big]\cdot\frac{\rho_{i}^{*}-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}}{\rho_{i}^{*}}\Bigg\}+o_{\varepsilon}(1)\varepsilon^{3}\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon}

Here,

DENOMINATOR

\displaystyle=h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}})\rho_{i}^{*}+\Big(h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}})L^{\varepsilon}_{i,t}+\sum_{s\neq t}{e^{H_{i,s}(p_{\varepsilon})-H_{i,t}(p_{\varepsilon})}h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}L^{\varepsilon}_{i,s}

\displaystyle\quad+o_{\delta}(1)\Big)\varepsilon_{t}^{2}\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}+o_{\varepsilon}(1)\varepsilon_{t}^{2}\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}

(3.19)

and

\displaystyle L^{\varepsilon}_{i,s}=\Bigg|\frac{\nabla h_{i}(p_{s,\varepsilon})}{h_{i}(p_{s,\varepsilon})}+8\pi\nabla_{1}{G^{*}}(p_{s,\varepsilon};p_{s,\varepsilon})+\nabla w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}(p_{s,\varepsilon})\Bigg|^{2}e^{I_{i}}+\Bigg(\frac{\Delta h_{i}(p_{s,\varepsilon})}{h_{i}(p_{s,\varepsilon})}+8N\pi\Bigg)e^{I_{i}}

for
s=1,\cdots,N
.
By a similar computation as in Lemma
3.4
, we get

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\Bigg[-\Delta(U_{\varepsilon}^{i}+\sum_{l=1}^{n}a^{il}w_{l,\varepsilon}^{I})-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\Big(\frac{h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}-1\Big)\Bigg]Z_{3,i,t,\varepsilon}^{*}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{2}{N}\Big(L^{\varepsilon}_{i,t}+\sum_{s\neq t}{\frac{e^{H_{i,s}(p_{\varepsilon})}}{e^{H_{i,t}(p_{\varepsilon})}}}L^{\varepsilon}_{i,s}+o_{\delta}(1)\Big)\varepsilon_{t}^{3}\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}+2\pi\Lambda_{I,N}(\rho_{\varepsilon})\varepsilon_{t}+o_{\varepsilon}(1)\varepsilon_{t}^{3}\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{2}{N}\Big(L_{i,t}+\sum_{s\neq t}{\frac{e^{H_{i,s}(p^{*})}}{e^{H_{i,t}(p^{*})}}}L_{i,s}+o_{\delta}(1)\Big)\varepsilon_{t}^{3}\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}+2\pi\Lambda_{I,N}(\rho_{\varepsilon})\varepsilon_{t}+o_{\varepsilon}(1)\varepsilon_{t}^{3}\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}.

Here,

\displaystyle L^{\varepsilon}_{i,s}=\Bigg|\frac{\nabla h_{i}(p_{s,\varepsilon})}{h_{i}(p_{s,\varepsilon})}+8\pi\nabla_{1}{G^{*}}(p_{s,\varepsilon};p_{s,\varepsilon})+\nabla w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}(p_{s,\varepsilon})\Bigg|^{2}e^{I_{i}}+\Bigg(\frac{\Delta h_{i}(p_{s,\varepsilon})}{h_{i}(p_{s,\varepsilon})}+8N\pi\Bigg)e^{I_{i}}

and

\displaystyle L_{i,s}=\Bigg|\frac{\nabla h_{i}(p_{s}^{*})}{h_{i}(p_{s}^{*})}+8\pi\nabla_{1}{G^{*}}(p_{s}^{*};p_{s}^{*})\Bigg|^{2}e^{I_{i}}+\Bigg(\frac{\Delta h_{i}(p_{s}^{*})}{h_{i}(p_{s}^{*})}+8N\pi\Bigg)e^{I_{i}}

(3.20)

for
i=1,\cdots,n
and
s=1,\cdots,N
.

\Box

Lemma 3.8

Assume that
m^{*}=4
and that

\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{t}^{N}{e^{H_{i,t}(p^{*})}}L_{i,t}=0
.
Here,
L_{i,s}
is defined as in (
3.20
). Then,

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{\Omega}\Bigg[-\Delta\Big(U_{\varepsilon}^{i}+\sum_{l=1}^{n}a^{il}w_{l,\varepsilon}^{I}\Big)-\rho_{i,\varepsilon}\Bigg(\frac{h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}{\int_{\Omega}h_{i}e^{U_{i,\varepsilon}+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}}}-1\Bigg)\Bigg]Z_{3,i,t,\varepsilon}^{*}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{2}{N}\Big(D_{i,t}+\sum_{s\neq t}{\frac{e^{H_{i,s}(p_{\varepsilon})}}{e^{H_{i,t}(p^{*})}}}D_{i,s}+o_{\delta}(1)\Big)\varepsilon_{t}^{3}+2\pi\Lambda_{I,N}(\rho_{\varepsilon})\varepsilon_{t}+o_{\varepsilon}(1)\varepsilon^{3}.

Here,

D_{i,s}:=\Bigg[\int_{\Omega_{t}}\frac{\frac{h_{i}(x)}{h_{i}(p_{t}^{*})}e^{8\pi({G^{*}}(x;p_{t,\varepsilon})-{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon}))}-1}{|x-p_{t}^{*}|^{4}}dx-\int_{\Omega_{t}^{c}}\frac{dx}{|x-p_{t}^{*}|^{4}}\Bigg]e^{I_{i}}

for
i=1,\cdots,n
and
s=1,\cdots,N
. The sub-domains
\Omega_{s}\subset\Omega
satisfy

•

\overline{\cup_{s=1}^{N}\Omega_{s}}=\overline{\Omega}
;

•

\Omega_{s}\cap\Omega_{s^{\prime}}=\emptyset
for any
s,s^{\prime}=1,\cdots,N
with
s\neq s^{\prime}
;

•

B_{3\delta_{s}}(p_{s,\varepsilon_{s}})\in\Omega_{s}
for any
s=1,\cdots,N
.

Proof.

Since a lot of the computations are similar to Lemmas
3.4
and
3.7
, we only sketch the proof.
It is only need to prove that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}

\displaystyle\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}})}\Bigg\{\frac{h_{i}(x)}{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}})}e^{8\pi({G^{*}}(x;p_{t,\varepsilon})-{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon}))+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}+(1-\chi_{t})(W^{**}_{i,\varepsilon}-W^{*}_{i,\varepsilon})}-1\Bigg\}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}dx

\displaystyle=\varepsilon^{2}\Bigg(e^{I_{i}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}})}\frac{\frac{h_{i}(x)}{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}})}e^{8\pi({G^{*}}(x;p_{t,\varepsilon})-{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon}))}-1}{|x-p_{t}^{*}|^{4}}dx+o_{\varepsilon}(1)\Bigg).

Notice that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}

\displaystyle\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon_{t}})}\Bigg\{\frac{h_{i}(x)}{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}e^{8\pi({G^{*}}(x;p_{t,\varepsilon})-{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon}))+w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}+(1-\chi_{t})(W^{**}_{i,\varepsilon}-W^{*}_{i,\varepsilon})}-1\Bigg\}e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}dx

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{B_{\delta_{t}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})\backslash B_{\varepsilon_{t}^{\frac{4}{5}}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}}+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{B_{\varepsilon_{t}^{\frac{4}{5}}}}\Bigg\{\frac{h_{i}(x)}{h_{i}(p_{t,\varepsilon})}e^{8\pi({G^{*}}(x;p_{t,\varepsilon})-{G^{*}}(p_{t,\varepsilon};p_{t,\varepsilon}))}e^{w_{i,\varepsilon}^{I}+(1-\chi_{t})(W^{**}_{i,\varepsilon}-W^{*}_{i,\varepsilon})}-1\Bigg\}\times

\displaystyle\quad\times e^{v_{i}(\frac{x-p_{t,\varepsilon}}{\varepsilon_{t}})+2\ln\frac{1}{\varepsilon_{t}}}dx

\displaystyle=:II^{\prime}_{1}+II^{\prime}_{2}.

Here,
